
=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Backend/InventoryItemController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Backend;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Division;
use App\Models\InventoryItem;
use App\Models\Outlet;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class InventoryItemController extends Controller
{
    private const UNITS = ['pcs', 'butir', 'gr', 'kg', 'ml', 'liter', 'cm', 'pack', 'box', 'tray', 'botol', 'cup', 'slice', 'loaf', 'porsi'];

    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        $filters = $request->validate([
            'search' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'division_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:divisions,id'],
            'item_type' => ['nullable', Rule::in(InventoryItem::TYPES)],
            'status' => ['nullable', Rule::in(['active', 'inactive'])],
        ]);

        return view('backend.inventory-items.index', [
            'judul' => 'Inventory Item',
            'items' => InventoryItem::with('division')
                ->when($filters['search'] ?? null, fn ($query, $search) => $query->where(function ($inner) use ($search) {
                    $inner->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")
                        ->orWhere('code', 'like', "%{$search}%");
                }))
                ->when($filters['division_id'] ?? null, fn ($query, $divisionId) => $query->where('division_id', $divisionId))
                ->when($filters['item_type'] ?? null, fn ($query, $type) => $query->where('item_type', $type))
                ->when($filters['status'] ?? null, fn ($query, $status) => $query->where('is_active', $status === 'active'))
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(20)
                ->withQueryString(),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'itemTypes' => InventoryItem::TYPES,
        ]);
    }

    public function create()
    {
        return view('backend.inventory-items.create', [
            'judul' => 'Tambah Inventory Item',
            'item' => new InventoryItem(['is_active' => true, 'min_stock' => 0]),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'units' => self::UNITS,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        $item = InventoryItem::create($this->validated($request));

        Outlet::where('is_active', true)->get()->each(fn ($outlet) => $item->stocks()->create([
            'outlet_id' => $outlet->id,
            'unit' => $item->base_unit,
        ]));

        return redirect()->route('backend.inventory-items.index')->with('success', 'Inventory item berhasil dibuat.');
    }

    public function edit(InventoryItem $inventoryItem)
    {
        return view('backend.inventory-items.edit', [
            'judul' => 'Edit Inventory Item',
            'item' => $inventoryItem,
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'units' => self::UNITS,
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, InventoryItem $inventoryItem)
    {
        $inventoryItem->update($this->validated($request, $inventoryItem));

        return redirect()->route('backend.inventory-items.index')->with('success', 'Inventory item berhasil diperbarui.');
    }

    public function destroy(InventoryItem $inventoryItem)
    {
        $inventoryItem->delete();

        return redirect()->route('backend.inventory-items.index')->with('success', 'Inventory item berhasil dihapus.');
    }

    private function validated(Request $request, ?InventoryItem $item = null): array
    {
        return $request->validate([
            'division_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:divisions,id'],
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'code' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255', Rule::unique('inventory_items', 'code')->ignore($item)],
            'item_type' => ['required', Rule::in(InventoryItem::TYPES)],
            'base_unit' => ['required', Rule::in(self::UNITS)],
            'purchase_unit' => ['nullable', Rule::in(self::UNITS)],
            'conversion_value' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'min_stock' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'max_stock' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'lead_time_days' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'min:0'],
            'default_supplier_name' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'last_purchase_price' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'is_active' => ['nullable', 'boolean'],
            'notes' => ['nullable', 'string'],
        ]) + ['is_active' => false, 'lead_time_days' => 0];
    }
}


=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Backend/InventoryStockController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Backend;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\InventoryStock;
use App\Models\Outlet;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class InventoryStockController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        return view('backend.inventory-stocks.index', [
            'judul' => 'Stok Outlet',
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'stocks' => InventoryStock::with(['outlet', 'item.division'])
                ->when($request->filled('outlet_id'), fn ($query) => $query->where('outlet_id', $request->outlet_id))
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(30)
                ->withQueryString(),
        ]);
    }

    public function edit(InventoryStock $inventoryStock)
    {
        return view('backend.inventory-stocks.edit', [
            'judul' => 'Edit Stok Outlet',
            'stock' => $inventoryStock->load(['outlet', 'item']),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, InventoryStock $inventoryStock)
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            'current_stock' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'reserved_stock' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'available_stock' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'unit' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
        ]);

        $inventoryStock->update($data);

        return redirect()->route('backend.inventory-stocks.index')->with('success', 'Stok outlet berhasil diperbarui.');
    }
}


=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Backend/MaterialRequestController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Backend;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\MaterialRequest;
use App\Models\MaterialRequestItem;
use App\Models\StockMovement;
use App\Services\Inventory\StockMovementService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Throwable;

class MaterialRequestController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private StockMovementService $stockMovementService)
    {
    }

    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        return view('backend.material-requests.index', [
            'judul' => 'Material Request',
            'requests' => MaterialRequest::with(['outlet', 'division', 'requester', 'items'])
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(20),
        ]);
    }

    public function show(MaterialRequest $materialRequest)
    {
        $materialRequest->load(['outlet', 'division', 'requester', 'dailyOperation', 'items.item']);
        $movementsByItemId = StockMovement::where('reference_type', MaterialRequestItem::class)
            ->whereIn('reference_id', $materialRequest->items->pluck('id'))
            ->get()
            ->keyBy('reference_id');

        return view('backend.material-requests.show', [
            'judul' => 'Detail Material Request',
            'materialRequest' => $materialRequest,
            'movementsByItemId' => $movementsByItemId,
        ]);
    }

    public function issueStock(Request $request, MaterialRequest $materialRequest)
    {
        if ($materialRequest->status !== 'submitted') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Hanya material request submitted yang bisa issue stock.');
        }

        $materialRequest->load(['items.item']);

        if ($materialRequest->items->isEmpty()) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Material request belum memiliki item.');
        }

        foreach ($materialRequest->items as $item) {
            if (! $item->item || $this->issueQuantity($item) <= 0) {
                return back()->with('error', 'Item material request tidak valid. Pastikan inventory item ada dan qty lebih dari 0.');
            }
        }

        try {
            $summary = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $materialRequest) {
                $summary = ['issued' => 0, 'duplicate' => 0];

                foreach ($materialRequest->items as $item) {
                    $result = $this->stockMovementService->decreaseStock([
                        'outlet_id' => $materialRequest->outlet_id,
                        'inventory_item_id' => $item->inventory_item_id,
                        'quantity' => $this->issueQuantity($item),
                        'movement_type' => 'material_issue_out',
                        'reference_type' => MaterialRequestItem::class,
                        'reference_id' => $item->id,
                        'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
                        'notes' => 'Material issue from material request ' . ($materialRequest->request_number ?? $materialRequest->id),
                    ]);

                    $result['duplicate'] ? $summary['duplicate']++ : $summary['issued']++;
                }

                $materialRequest->update(['status' => 'issued']);

                return $summary;
            });
        } catch (Throwable $exception) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Issue stock gagal: ' . $exception->getMessage());
        }

        return back()->with('success', "Material issue applied. Issued: {$summary['issued']}, duplicate ignored: {$summary['duplicate']}.");
    }

    private function issueQuantity(MaterialRequestItem $item): float
    {
        foreach (['issued_qty', 'approved_qty', 'requested_qty'] as $field) {
            if (is_numeric($item->{$field}) && (float) $item->{$field} > 0) {
                return (float) $item->{$field};
            }
        }

        return 0.0;
    }
}

=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Backend/StockOpnameController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Backend;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\StockMovement;
use App\Models\StockOpname;
use App\Models\StockOpnameItem;
use App\Services\Inventory\StockMovementService;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Throwable;

class StockOpnameController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private StockMovementService $stockMovementService)
    {
    }

    public function index()
    {
        return view('backend.stock-opnames.index', [
            'judul' => 'Stock Opname',
            'opnames' => StockOpname::with(['outlet', 'division', 'counter', 'items'])
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(20),
        ]);
    }

    public function show(StockOpname $stockOpname)
    {
        $stockOpname->load(['outlet', 'division', 'counter', 'reviewer', 'dailyOperation', 'items.item']);
        $movementsByItemId = StockMovement::where('reference_type', StockOpnameItem::class)
            ->whereIn('reference_id', $stockOpname->items->pluck('id'))
            ->get()
            ->keyBy('reference_id');

        return view('backend.stock-opnames.show', [
            'judul' => 'Detail Stock Opname',
            'stockOpname' => $stockOpname,
            'movementsByItemId' => $movementsByItemId,
        ]);
    }

    public function review(Request $request, StockOpname $stockOpname)
    {
        if ($stockOpname->status !== 'submitted') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Hanya stock opname submitted yang bisa direview.');
        }

        $stockOpname->load(['items.item']);

        if ($stockOpname->items->isEmpty()) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Stock opname belum memiliki item.');
        }

        foreach ($stockOpname->items as $item) {
            if (! $item->item || ! is_numeric($item->physical_stock) || (float) $item->physical_stock < 0) {
                return back()->with('error', 'Item opname tidak valid. Pastikan inventory item ada dan physical stock tidak negatif.');
            }
        }

        try {
            $summary = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $stockOpname) {
                $summary = ['adjusted' => 0, 'noop' => 0, 'duplicate' => 0];

                foreach ($stockOpname->items as $item) {
                    $result = $this->stockMovementService->adjustStock([
                        'outlet_id' => $stockOpname->outlet_id,
                        'inventory_item_id' => $item->inventory_item_id,
                        'new_stock' => $item->physical_stock,
                        'movement_type' => 'stock_opname_adjustment',
                        'reference_type' => StockOpnameItem::class,
                        'reference_id' => $item->id,
                        'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
                        'notes' => 'Adjustment from reviewed stock opname ' . ($stockOpname->opname_number ?? $stockOpname->id),
                    ]);

                    if ($result['duplicate']) {
                        $summary['duplicate']++;
                    } elseif ($result['noop']) {
                        $summary['noop']++;
                    } else {
                        $summary['adjusted']++;
                    }
                }

                $stockOpname->update([
                    'status' => 'reviewed',
                    'reviewed_by' => $request->user()->id,
                    'reviewed_at' => now(),
                ]);

                return $summary;
            });
        } catch (Throwable $exception) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Review gagal: ' . $exception->getMessage());
        }

        return back()->with('success', "Stock opname direview. Adjustment: {$summary['adjusted']}, no-op: {$summary['noop']}, duplicate ignored: {$summary['duplicate']}.");
    }
}


=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Backend/WasteRecordController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Backend;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\StockMovement;
use App\Models\WasteRecord;
use App\Models\WasteRecordItem;
use App\Services\Inventory\StockMovementService;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Throwable;

class WasteRecordController extends Controller
{
    public function __construct(private StockMovementService $stockMovementService)
    {
    }

    public function index()
    {
        return view('backend.waste-records.index', [
            'judul' => 'Waste Record',
            'records' => WasteRecord::with(['outlet', 'division', 'recorder', 'items'])
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(20),
        ]);
    }

    public function show(WasteRecord $wasteRecord)
    {
        $wasteRecord->load(['outlet', 'division', 'recorder', 'dailyOperation', 'items.item']);
        $movementsByItemId = StockMovement::where('reference_type', WasteRecordItem::class)
            ->whereIn('reference_id', $wasteRecord->items->pluck('id'))
            ->get()
            ->keyBy('reference_id');

        return view('backend.waste-records.show', [
            'judul' => 'Detail Waste Record',
            'wasteRecord' => $wasteRecord,
            'movementsByItemId' => $movementsByItemId,
        ]);
    }

    public function applyStock(Request $request, WasteRecord $wasteRecord)
    {
        if ($wasteRecord->status !== 'submitted') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Hanya waste record submitted yang bisa apply stock.');
        }

        $wasteRecord->load(['items.item']);

        if ($wasteRecord->items->isEmpty()) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Waste record belum memiliki item.');
        }

        foreach ($wasteRecord->items as $item) {
            if (! $item->item || ! is_numeric($item->qty) || (float) $item->qty <= 0) {
                return back()->with('error', 'Item waste tidak valid. Pastikan inventory item ada dan qty lebih dari 0.');
            }
        }

        try {
            $summary = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $wasteRecord) {
                $summary = ['deducted' => 0, 'duplicate' => 0];

                foreach ($wasteRecord->items as $item) {
                    $result = $this->stockMovementService->decreaseStock([
                        'outlet_id' => $wasteRecord->outlet_id,
                        'inventory_item_id' => $item->inventory_item_id,
                        'quantity' => $item->qty,
                        'movement_type' => 'waste_out',
                        'reference_type' => WasteRecordItem::class,
                        'reference_id' => $item->id,
                        'user_id' => $request->user()->id,
                        'notes' => 'Waste deduction from waste record ' . ($wasteRecord->waste_number ?? $wasteRecord->id),
                    ]);

                    $result['duplicate'] ? $summary['duplicate']++ : $summary['deducted']++;
                }

                $wasteRecord->update(['status' => 'applied']);

                return $summary;
            });
        } catch (Throwable $exception) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Apply stock gagal: ' . $exception->getMessage());
        }

        return back()->with('success', "Waste stock deduction applied. Deducted: {$summary['deducted']}, duplicate ignored: {$summary['duplicate']}.");
    }
}


=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Manager/InventoryMonitoringController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Manager;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Division;
use App\Models\DivisionDailyOperation;
use App\Models\InventoryStock;
use App\Models\MaterialRequest;
use App\Models\Outlet;
use App\Models\ProductionRecord;
use App\Models\StockOpname;
use App\Models\WasteRecord;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class InventoryMonitoringController extends Controller
{
    public function inventory(Request $request)
    {
        return view('manager.inventory.index', [
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'stocks' => InventoryStock::with(['outlet', 'item.division'])
                ->when($request->filled('outlet_id'), fn ($query) => $query->where('outlet_id', $request->outlet_id))
                ->paginate(30)
                ->withQueryString(),
        ]);
    }

    public function dailyOperations()
    {
        return view('manager.daily-operations.index', [
            'operations' => DivisionDailyOperation::with(['outlet', 'division', 'opener', 'submitter'])
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(30),
        ]);
    }

    public function dailyOperationShow(DivisionDailyOperation $dailyOperation)
    {
        return view('manager.daily-operations.show', [
            'operation' => $dailyOperation->load(['outlet', 'division', 'opener', 'submitter', 'approver']),
            'summary' => $this->summary($dailyOperation),
        ]);
    }

    public function openingAlerts(Request $request)
    {
        $outletId = $request->integer('outlet_id') ?: Outlet::where('is_active', true)->value('id');
        $divisionId = $request->integer('division_id') ?: Division::where('is_active', true)->value('id');

        $alerts = InventoryStock::with(['item.division', 'outlet'])
            ->where('outlet_id', $outletId)
            ->whereHas('item', fn ($query) => $query
                ->where('is_active', true)
                ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
                ->whereIn('item_type', ['wip', 'raw_material', 'packaging']))
            ->get()
            ->filter(fn ($stock) => (float) $stock->current_stock <= (float) $stock->item->min_stock);

        return view('manager.opening-alerts.index', [
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'selectedOutletId' => $outletId,
            'selectedDivisionId' => $divisionId,
            'alerts' => $alerts,
        ]);
    }

    private function summary(DivisionDailyOperation $operation): array
    {
        $id = $operation->id;

        return [
            'materialRequests' => MaterialRequest::with('items')->where('division_daily_operation_id', $id)->latest()->get(),
            'productionRecords' => ProductionRecord::with('items')->where('division_daily_operation_id', $id)->latest()->get(),
            'wasteRecords' => WasteRecord::with('items')->where('division_daily_operation_id', $id)->latest()->get(),
            'stockOpnames' => StockOpname::with('items')->where('division_daily_operation_id', $id)->latest()->get(),
        ];
    }
}

=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Staff/MaterialRequestController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Staff;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Division;
use App\Models\DivisionDailyOperation;
use App\Models\InventoryItem;
use App\Models\InventoryStock;
use App\Models\MaterialRequest;
use App\Models\Outlet;
use App\Models\UserDivisionAccess;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class MaterialRequestController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request);

        $requests = $message
            ? MaterialRequest::whereRaw('1 = 0')->paginate(15)
            : MaterialRequest::with(['outlet', 'division', 'requester', 'items'])
                ->where('outlet_id', $outletId)
                ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(15)
                ->withQueryString();

        return view('staff.material-requests.index', [
            'message' => $message,
            'requests' => $requests,
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'selectedOutletId' => $outletId,
            'selectedDivisionId' => $divisionId,
        ]);
    }

    public function create(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request);
        $operation = $this->openOperation($outletId, $divisionId);

        $items = collect();
        $stocks = collect();
        if (! $message && $operation?->status === 'open') {
            $items = InventoryItem::where('is_active', true)
                ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
                ->whereIn('item_type', ['raw_material', 'wip', 'packaging'])
                ->orderBy('name')
                ->get();

            $stocks = InventoryStock::where('outlet_id', $outletId)
                ->whereIn('inventory_item_id', $items->pluck('id'))
                ->get()
                ->keyBy('inventory_item_id');
        }

        return view('staff.material-requests.create', [
            'message' => $message,
            'operation' => $operation,
            'items' => $items,
            'stocks' => $stocks,
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'selectedOutletId' => $outletId,
            'selectedDivisionId' => $divisionId,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request, true);
        if ($message || ! $outletId || ! $divisionId) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Akun staff belum memiliki akses outlet/divisi.');
        }

        $operation = $this->openOperation($outletId, $divisionId);
        if (! $operation || $operation->status !== 'open') {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Buka Operasional Hari Ini terlebih dahulu sebelum membuat Take Bahan.');
        }

        $data = $request->validate([
            'notes' => ['nullable', 'string'],
            'items' => ['required', 'array'],
            'items.*.inventory_item_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:inventory_items,id'],
            'items.*.requested_qty' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0.01'],
            'items.*.unit' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'items.*.notes' => ['nullable', 'string'],
        ]);

        $rows = collect($data['items'])
            ->filter(fn ($row) => ! empty($row['inventory_item_id']) && ! empty($row['requested_qty']))
            ->values();

        if ($rows->isEmpty()) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Isi minimal satu item Take Bahan.');
        }

        $validItemIds = InventoryItem::where('is_active', true)
            ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
            ->whereIn('item_type', ['raw_material', 'wip', 'packaging'])
            ->pluck('id')
            ->all();

        foreach ($rows as $row) {
            if (! in_array((int) $row['inventory_item_id'], $validItemIds, true)) {
                return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Item harus aktif dan sesuai divisi.');
            }
        }

        $materialRequest = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $rows, $operation, $outletId, $divisionId, $data) {
            $materialRequest = MaterialRequest::create([
                'division_daily_operation_id' => $operation->id,
                'outlet_id' => $outletId,
                'division_id' => $divisionId,
                'requested_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'request_date' => today(),
                'status' => 'draft',
                'notes' => $data['notes'] ?? null,
            ]);

            $outletCode = $operation->outlet?->code ?? 'OUT';
            $divisionCode = $operation->division?->code ?? 'DIV';
            $materialRequest->update([
                'request_number' => sprintf('MR-%s-%s-%s-%03d', $outletCode, $divisionCode, today()->format('Ymd'), $materialRequest->id),
            ]);

            foreach ($rows as $row) {
                $item = InventoryItem::find($row['inventory_item_id']);
                $materialRequest->items()->create([
                    'inventory_item_id' => $item->id,
                    'requested_qty' => $row['requested_qty'],
                    'unit' => $row['unit'] ?: $item->base_unit,
                    'notes' => $row['notes'] ?? null,
                ]);
            }

            return $materialRequest;
        });

        return redirect()->route('staff.material-requests.show', $materialRequest)->with('success', 'Take Bahan berhasil dibuat.');
    }

    public function show(Request $request, MaterialRequest $materialRequest)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $materialRequest), 403);

        return view('staff.material-requests.show', [
            'materialRequest' => $materialRequest->load(['outlet', 'division', 'requester', 'dailyOperation', 'items.item']),
        ]);
    }

    public function submit(Request $request, MaterialRequest $materialRequest)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $materialRequest), 403);

        if ($materialRequest->status !== 'draft') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Hanya draft yang bisa disubmit.');
        }

        $materialRequest->update([
            'status' => 'submitted',
            'submitted_at' => now(),
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Take Bahan berhasil disubmit.');
    }

    public function cancel(Request $request, MaterialRequest $materialRequest)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $materialRequest), 403);

        if (! in_array($materialRequest->status, ['draft', 'submitted'], true)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Take Bahan ini tidak bisa dibatalkan.');
        }

        $materialRequest->update(['status' => 'cancelled']);

        return back()->with('success', 'Take Bahan berhasil dibatalkan.');
    }

    private function context(Request $request, bool $validateAdminInput = false): array
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if ($user->hasAnyRole(['admin', 'super_admin'])) {
            if ($validateAdminInput) {
                $data = $request->validate([
                    'outlet_id' => ['required', 'exists:outlets,id'],
                    'division_id' => ['required', 'exists:divisions,id'],
                ]);

                return [(int) $data['outlet_id'], (int) $data['division_id'], null];
            }

            return [
                $request->integer('outlet_id') ?: Outlet::where('is_active', true)->value('id'),
                $request->integer('division_id') ?: Division::where('is_active', true)->value('id'),
                null,
            ];
        }

        $access = UserDivisionAccess::where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->where(function ($query) {
                $query->where('can_request_order', true)
                    ->orWhere('can_input_stock', true);
            })
            ->first();

        if (! $access) {
            return [null, null, 'Akun staff belum memiliki akses outlet/divisi.'];
        }

        return [$access->outlet_id, $access->division_id, null];
    }

    private function openOperation(?int $outletId, ?int $divisionId): ?DivisionDailyOperation
    {
        if (! $outletId || ! $divisionId) {
            return null;
        }

        return DivisionDailyOperation::with(['outlet', 'division'])
            ->where('outlet_id', $outletId)
            ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
            ->whereDate('operation_date', today())
            ->first();
    }

    private function canAccess(Request $request, MaterialRequest $materialRequest): bool
    {
        return $request->user()->hasAnyRole(['admin', 'super_admin'])
            || $request->user()->hasDivisionAccess($materialRequest->outlet_id, $materialRequest->division_id, 'can_request_order')
            || $request->user()->hasDivisionAccess($materialRequest->outlet_id, $materialRequest->division_id, 'can_input_stock');
    }
}


=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Staff/StockOpnameController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Staff;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Division;
use App\Models\DivisionDailyOperation;
use App\Models\InventoryItem;
use App\Models\InventoryStock;
use App\Models\Outlet;
use App\Models\StockOpname;
use App\Models\UserDivisionAccess;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class StockOpnameController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request);

        $opnames = $message
            ? StockOpname::whereRaw('1 = 0')->paginate(15)
            : StockOpname::with(['outlet', 'division', 'counter', 'items'])
                ->where('outlet_id', $outletId)
                ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(15)
                ->withQueryString();

        return view('staff.stock-opnames.index', [
            'message' => $message,
            'opnames' => $opnames,
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'selectedOutletId' => $outletId,
            'selectedDivisionId' => $divisionId,
        ]);
    }

    public function create(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request);
        $operation = $this->openOperation($outletId, $divisionId);
        $search = $request->string('search')->toString();
        $itemType = $request->string('item_type')->toString();

        $items = collect();
        $stocks = collect();
        if (! $message && $operation?->status === 'open') {
            $items = InventoryItem::where('is_active', true)
                ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
                ->when($search, fn ($query) => $query->where(function ($inner) use ($search) {
                    $inner->where('name', 'like', "%{$search}%")->orWhere('code', 'like', "%{$search}%");
                }))
                ->when($itemType, fn ($query) => $query->where('item_type', $itemType))
                ->orderBy('name')
                ->get();

            $stocks = InventoryStock::where('outlet_id', $outletId)
                ->whereIn('inventory_item_id', $items->pluck('id'))
                ->get()
                ->keyBy('inventory_item_id');
        }

        return view('staff.stock-opnames.create', [
            'message' => $message,
            'operation' => $operation,
            'items' => $items,
            'stocks' => $stocks,
            'itemTypes' => InventoryItem::TYPES,
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'selectedOutletId' => $outletId,
            'selectedDivisionId' => $divisionId,
            'search' => $search,
            'itemType' => $itemType,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request, true);
        if ($message || ! $outletId || ! $divisionId) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Akun staff belum memiliki akses outlet/divisi.');
        }

        $operation = $this->openOperation($outletId, $divisionId);
        if (! $operation || $operation->status !== 'open') {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Buka Operasional Hari Ini terlebih dahulu sebelum Closing Stock Opname.');
        }

        $data = $request->validate([
            'notes' => ['nullable', 'string'],
            'items' => ['required', 'array'],
            'items.*.inventory_item_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:inventory_items,id'],
            'items.*.physical_stock' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'items.*.notes' => ['nullable', 'string'],
        ]);

        $rows = collect($data['items'])
            ->filter(fn ($row) => ! empty($row['inventory_item_id']) && $row['physical_stock'] !== null && $row['physical_stock'] !== '')
            ->values();

        if ($rows->isEmpty()) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Isi minimal satu physical stock.');
        }

        $ids = $rows->pluck('inventory_item_id')->map(fn ($id) => (int) $id);
        if ($ids->duplicates()->isNotEmpty()) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Item tidak boleh duplikat dalam satu stock opname.');
        }

        $validItemIds = InventoryItem::where('is_active', true)
            ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
            ->pluck('id')
            ->all();

        foreach ($ids as $id) {
            if (! in_array($id, $validItemIds, true)) {
                return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Item harus aktif dan sesuai divisi.');
            }
        }

        $stockOpname = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $rows, $operation, $outletId, $divisionId, $data) {
            $stockOpname = StockOpname::create([
                'division_daily_operation_id' => $operation->id,
                'outlet_id' => $outletId,
                'division_id' => $divisionId,
                'counted_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'opname_date' => today(),
                'opname_type' => 'closing',
                'status' => 'draft',
                'notes' => $data['notes'] ?? null,
            ]);

            $outletCode = $operation->outlet?->code ?? 'OUT';
            $divisionCode = $operation->division?->code ?? 'DIV';
            $stockOpname->update([
                'opname_number' => sprintf('SO-%s-%s-%s-%03d', $outletCode, $divisionCode, today()->format('Ymd'), $stockOpname->id),
            ]);

            foreach ($rows as $row) {
                $item = InventoryItem::find($row['inventory_item_id']);
                $stock = InventoryStock::where('outlet_id', $outletId)->where('inventory_item_id', $item->id)->first();
                $systemStock = (float) ($stock?->current_stock ?? 0);
                $physicalStock = (float) $row['physical_stock'];

                $stockOpname->items()->create([
                    'inventory_item_id' => $item->id,
                    'system_stock' => $systemStock,
                    'physical_stock' => $physicalStock,
                    'difference_qty' => $physicalStock - $systemStock,
                    'unit' => $stock?->unit ?? $item->base_unit,
                    'notes' => $row['notes'] ?? null,
                ]);
            }

            return $stockOpname;
        });

        return redirect()->route('staff.stock-opnames.show', $stockOpname)->with('success', 'Closing Stock Opname berhasil dibuat.');
    }

    public function show(Request $request, StockOpname $stockOpname)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $stockOpname), 403);

        return view('staff.stock-opnames.show', [
            'stockOpname' => $stockOpname->load(['outlet', 'division', 'counter', 'reviewer', 'dailyOperation', 'items.item']),
        ]);
    }

    public function submit(Request $request, StockOpname $stockOpname)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $stockOpname), 403);

        if ($stockOpname->status !== 'draft') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Hanya draft yang bisa disubmit.');
        }

        $stockOpname->update(['status' => 'submitted', 'submitted_at' => now()]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Closing Stock Opname berhasil disubmit.');
    }

    public function cancel(Request $request, StockOpname $stockOpname)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $stockOpname), 403);

        if (! in_array($stockOpname->status, ['draft', 'submitted'], true)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Stock opname ini tidak bisa dibatalkan.');
        }

        $stockOpname->update(['status' => 'cancelled', 'cancelled_at' => now()]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Closing Stock Opname berhasil dibatalkan.');
    }

    private function context(Request $request, bool $validateAdminInput = false): array
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if ($user->hasAnyRole(['admin', 'super_admin'])) {
            if ($validateAdminInput) {
                $data = $request->validate([
                    'outlet_id' => ['required', 'exists:outlets,id'],
                    'division_id' => ['required', 'exists:divisions,id'],
                ]);

                return [(int) $data['outlet_id'], (int) $data['division_id'], null];
            }

            return [
                $request->integer('outlet_id') ?: Outlet::where('is_active', true)->value('id'),
                $request->integer('division_id') ?: Division::where('is_active', true)->value('id'),
                null,
            ];
        }

        $access = UserDivisionAccess::where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->where('can_input_stock', true)
            ->first();

        if (! $access) {
            return [null, null, 'Akun staff belum memiliki akses outlet/divisi.'];
        }

        return [$access->outlet_id, $access->division_id, null];
    }

    private function openOperation(?int $outletId, ?int $divisionId): ?DivisionDailyOperation
    {
        if (! $outletId || ! $divisionId) {
            return null;
        }

        return DivisionDailyOperation::with(['outlet', 'division'])
            ->where('outlet_id', $outletId)
            ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
            ->whereDate('operation_date', today())
            ->first();
    }

    private function canAccess(Request $request, StockOpname $stockOpname): bool
    {
        return $request->user()->hasAnyRole(['admin', 'super_admin'])
            || $request->user()->hasDivisionAccess($stockOpname->outlet_id, $stockOpname->division_id, 'can_input_stock');
    }
}


=== FILE: app/Http/Controllers/Staff/WasteRecordController.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Staff;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\Division;
use App\Models\DivisionDailyOperation;
use App\Models\InventoryItem;
use App\Models\InventoryStock;
use App\Models\Outlet;
use App\Models\UserDivisionAccess;
use App\Models\WasteRecord;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class WasteRecordController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request);

        $records = $message
            ? WasteRecord::whereRaw('1 = 0')->paginate(15)
            : WasteRecord::with(['outlet', 'division', 'recorder', 'items'])
                ->where('outlet_id', $outletId)
                ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
                ->latest()
                ->paginate(15)
                ->withQueryString();

        return view('staff.waste.index', [
            'message' => $message,
            'records' => $records,
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'selectedOutletId' => $outletId,
            'selectedDivisionId' => $divisionId,
        ]);
    }

    public function create(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request);
        $operation = $this->openOperation($outletId, $divisionId);

        $items = collect();
        $stocks = collect();
        if (! $message && $operation?->status === 'open') {
            $items = InventoryItem::where('is_active', true)
                ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
                ->whereIn('item_type', InventoryItem::TYPES)
                ->orderBy('name')
                ->get();

            $stocks = InventoryStock::where('outlet_id', $outletId)
                ->whereIn('inventory_item_id', $items->pluck('id'))
                ->get()
                ->keyBy('inventory_item_id');
        }

        return view('staff.waste.create', [
            'message' => $message,
            'operation' => $operation,
            'items' => $items,
            'stocks' => $stocks,
            'reasons' => WasteRecord::REASONS,
            'outlets' => Outlet::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'divisions' => Division::where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get(),
            'selectedOutletId' => $outletId,
            'selectedDivisionId' => $divisionId,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request)
    {
        [$outletId, $divisionId, $message] = $this->context($request, true);
        if ($message || ! $outletId || ! $divisionId) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Akun staff belum memiliki akses outlet/divisi.');
        }

        $operation = $this->openOperation($outletId, $divisionId);
        if (! $operation || $operation->status !== 'open') {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Buka Operasional Hari Ini terlebih dahulu sebelum mencatat Waste.');
        }

        $data = $request->validate([
            'notes' => ['nullable', 'string'],
            'items' => ['required', 'array'],
            'items.*.inventory_item_id' => ['nullable', 'exists:inventory_items,id'],
            'items.*.qty' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0.01'],
            'items.*.unit' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:50'],
            'items.*.estimated_value' => ['nullable', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'items.*.reason' => ['nullable', Rule::in(WasteRecord::REASONS)],
            'items.*.notes' => ['nullable', 'string'],
        ]);

        $rows = collect($data['items'])
            ->filter(fn ($row) => ! empty($row['inventory_item_id']) && ! empty($row['qty']))
            ->values();

        if ($rows->isEmpty()) {
            return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Isi minimal satu item waste.');
        }

        $validItemIds = InventoryItem::where('is_active', true)
            ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
            ->whereIn('item_type', InventoryItem::TYPES)
            ->pluck('id')
            ->all();

        foreach ($rows as $row) {
            if (! in_array((int) $row['inventory_item_id'], $validItemIds, true) || empty($row['reason'])) {
                return back()->withInput()->with('error', 'Item harus aktif, sesuai divisi, dan alasan waste wajib diisi.');
            }
        }

        $wasteRecord = DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $rows, $operation, $outletId, $divisionId, $data) {
            $wasteRecord = WasteRecord::create([
                'division_daily_operation_id' => $operation->id,
                'outlet_id' => $outletId,
                'division_id' => $divisionId,
                'recorded_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'waste_date' => today(),
                'status' => 'draft',
                'notes' => $data['notes'] ?? null,
            ]);

            $outletCode = $operation->outlet?->code ?? 'OUT';
            $divisionCode = $operation->division?->code ?? 'DIV';
            $wasteRecord->update([
                'waste_number' => sprintf('WR-%s-%s-%s-%03d', $outletCode, $divisionCode, today()->format('Ymd'), $wasteRecord->id),
            ]);

            foreach ($rows as $row) {
                $item = InventoryItem::find($row['inventory_item_id']);
                $wasteRecord->items()->create([
                    'inventory_item_id' => $item->id,
                    'item_name' => $item->name,
                    'item_type' => $item->item_type,
                    'qty' => $row['qty'],
                    'unit' => $row['unit'] ?: $item->base_unit,
                    'estimated_value' => $row['estimated_value'] ?? null,
                    'reason' => $row['reason'],
                    'notes' => $row['notes'] ?? null,
                ]);
            }

            return $wasteRecord;
        });

        return redirect()->route('staff.waste.show', $wasteRecord)->with('success', 'Waste record berhasil dibuat.');
    }

    public function show(Request $request, WasteRecord $wasteRecord)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $wasteRecord), 403);

        return view('staff.waste.show', [
            'wasteRecord' => $wasteRecord->load(['outlet', 'division', 'recorder', 'dailyOperation', 'items.item']),
        ]);
    }

    public function submit(Request $request, WasteRecord $wasteRecord)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $wasteRecord), 403);

        if ($wasteRecord->status !== 'draft') {
            return back()->with('error', 'Hanya draft yang bisa disubmit.');
        }

        $wasteRecord->update([
            'status' => 'submitted',
            'submitted_at' => now(),
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Waste record berhasil disubmit.');
    }

    public function cancel(Request $request, WasteRecord $wasteRecord)
    {
        abort_unless($this->canAccess($request, $wasteRecord), 403);

        if (! in_array($wasteRecord->status, ['draft', 'submitted'], true)) {
            return back()->with('error', 'Waste record ini tidak bisa dibatalkan.');
        }

        $wasteRecord->update([
            'status' => 'cancelled',
            'cancelled_at' => now(),
        ]);

        return back()->with('success', 'Waste record berhasil dibatalkan.');
    }

    private function context(Request $request, bool $validateAdminInput = false): array
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if ($user->hasAnyRole(['admin', 'super_admin'])) {
            if ($validateAdminInput) {
                $data = $request->validate([
                    'outlet_id' => ['required', 'exists:outlets,id'],
                    'division_id' => ['required', 'exists:divisions,id'],
                ]);

                return [(int) $data['outlet_id'], (int) $data['division_id'], null];
            }

            return [
                $request->integer('outlet_id') ?: Outlet::where('is_active', true)->value('id'),
                $request->integer('division_id') ?: Division::where('is_active', true)->value('id'),
                null,
            ];
        }

        $access = UserDivisionAccess::where('user_id', $user->id)
            ->where(function ($query) {
                $query->where('can_input_waste', true)
                    ->orWhere('can_input_stock', true);
            })
            ->first();

        if (! $access) {
            return [null, null, 'Akun staff belum memiliki akses outlet/divisi.'];
        }

        return [$access->outlet_id, $access->division_id, null];
    }

    private function openOperation(?int $outletId, ?int $divisionId): ?DivisionDailyOperation
    {
        if (! $outletId || ! $divisionId) {
            return null;
        }

        return DivisionDailyOperation::with(['outlet', 'division'])
            ->where('outlet_id', $outletId)
            ->where('division_id', $divisionId)
            ->whereDate('operation_date', today())
            ->first();
    }

    private function canAccess(Request $request, WasteRecord $wasteRecord): bool
    {
        return $request->user()->hasAnyRole(['admin', 'super_admin'])
            || $request->user()->hasDivisionAccess($wasteRecord->outlet_id, $wasteRecord->division_id, 'can_input_waste')
            || $request->user()->hasDivisionAccess($wasteRecord->outlet_id, $wasteRecord->division_id, 'can_input_stock');
    }
}


=== FILE: app/Models/InventoryItem.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

class InventoryItem extends Model
{
    use HasFactory;

=== FILE: app/Services/Inventory/StockMovementService.php ===

<?php

namespace App\Services\Inventory;

use App\Models\InventoryItem;
use App\Models\InventoryStock;
use App\Models\StockMovement;
use DomainException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use InvalidArgumentException;

/**
 * Service pusat untuk perubahan stok inventory.
 *
 * Controller modul inventory/purchasing/produksi memakai service ini agar
 * update inventory_stocks dan pencatatan stock_movements selalu konsisten.
 */
class StockMovementService
{
    public function increaseStock(array $data): array
    {
        return $this->apply($data, 'increase');
    }

    public function decreaseStock(array $data): array
    {
        return $this->apply($data, 'decrease');
    }

    public function adjustStock(array $data): array
    {
        // Adjustment dipakai untuk opname/review ketika stok fisik menjadi angka baru.
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($data) {
            $outletId = $this->requiredInt($data, 'outlet_id');
            $itemId = $this->requiredInt($data, 'inventory_item_id');
            $newStock = $this->decimal($data['new_stock'] ?? null, 'new_stock');
            $movementType = $data['movement_type'] ?? 'stock_opname_adjustment';

            if ($newStock < 0) {
                throw new InvalidArgumentException('New stock must be greater than or equal to 0.');
            }

            $item = InventoryItem::findOrFail($itemId);
            $stock = $this->lockedStock($outletId, $item);
            $duplicate = $this->duplicateMovement($stock, $movementType, $data);

            if ($duplicate) {
                return $this->result($stock, $duplicate, true);
            }

            $before = (float) $stock->current_stock;
            $difference = $newStock - $before;

            if (abs($difference) < 0.00001) {
                return $this->result($stock, null, false, true);
            }

            $this->updateStock($stock, $newStock);

            $movement = $this->createMovement($stock, $item, [
                ...$data,
                'movement_type' => $movementType,
                'quantity' => abs($difference),
                'stock_before' => $before,
                'stock_after' => $newStock,
            ]);

            return $this->result($stock->fresh(), $movement);
        });
    }

    public function moveStock(array $data): array
    {
        // Transfer dibuat sebagai dua movement: keluar dari outlet asal dan masuk ke outlet tujuan.
        $quantity = $this->decimal($data['quantity'] ?? null, 'quantity');

        if ($quantity <= 0) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('Quantity must be greater than 0.');
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $quantity) {
            $out = $this->decreaseStock([
                ...$data,
                'quantity' => $quantity,
                'outlet_id' => $this->requiredInt($data, 'from_outlet_id'),
                'movement_type' => 'transfer_out',
            ]);

            $in = $this->increaseStock([
                ...$data,
                'quantity' => $quantity,
                'outlet_id' => $this->requiredInt($data, 'to_outlet_id'),
                'movement_type' => 'transfer_in',
            ]);

            return ['out' => $out, 'in' => $in];
        });
    }

    private function apply(array $data, string $direction): array
    {
        // Semua increase/decrease dibungkus transaction agar stok dan movement tidak terpisah.
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $direction) {
            $outletId = $this->requiredInt($data, 'outlet_id');
            $itemId = $this->requiredInt($data, 'inventory_item_id');
            $quantity = $this->decimal($data['quantity'] ?? null, 'quantity');
            $movementType = $data['movement_type'] ?? ($direction === 'increase' ? 'manual_adjustment' : 'material_issue_out');

            if ($quantity <= 0) {
                throw new InvalidArgumentException('Quantity must be greater than 0.');
            }

            $item = InventoryItem::findOrFail($itemId);
            $stock = $this->lockedStock($outletId, $item);
            $duplicate = $this->duplicateMovement($stock, $movementType, $data);

            if ($duplicate) {
                // Reference type/id mencegah proses apply stock yang sama menambah stok dua kali.
                return $this->result($stock, $duplicate, true);
            }

            $before = (float) $stock->current_stock;
            $after = $direction === 'increase' ? $before + $quantity : $before - $quantity;

            if ($after < 0 && ! ($data['allow_negative'] ?? false)) {
                throw new DomainException("Insufficient stock for item {$item->name}. Current stock: {$before}, requested: {$quantity}.");
            }

            $this->updateStock($stock, $after);

            $movement = $this->createMovement($stock, $item, [
                ...$data,
                'movement_type' => $movementType,
                'quantity' => $quantity,
                'stock_before' => $before,
                'stock_after' => $after,
            ]);

            return $this->result($stock->fresh(), $movement);
        });
    }

    private function lockedStock(int $outletId, InventoryItem $item): InventoryStock
    {
        // Lock stok per outlet+item supaya transaksi bersamaan tidak saling menimpa.
        $stock = InventoryStock::where('outlet_id', $outletId)
            ->where('inventory_item_id', $item->id)
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->first();

        if ($stock) {
            return $stock;
        }

        InventoryStock::create([
            'outlet_id' => $outletId,
            'inventory_item_id' => $item->id,
            'current_stock' => 0,
            'reserved_stock' => 0,
            'available_stock' => 0,
            'unit' => $item->base_unit,
        ]);

        return InventoryStock::where('outlet_id', $outletId)
            ->where('inventory_item_id', $item->id)
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->firstOrFail();
    }

    private function duplicateMovement(InventoryStock $stock, string $movementType, array $data): ?StockMovement
    {
        if (($data['allow_duplicate'] ?? false) || empty($data['reference_type']) || empty($data['reference_id'])) {
            return null;
        }

        return StockMovement::where('outlet_id', $stock->outlet_id)
            ->where('inventory_item_id', $stock->inventory_item_id)
            ->where('movement_type', $movementType)
            ->where('reference_type', $data['reference_type'])
            ->where('reference_id', $data['reference_id'])
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->first();
    }

    private function updateStock(InventoryStock $stock, float $currentStock): void
    {
        $reserved = (float) ($stock->reserved_stock ?? 0);

        $stock->update([
            'current_stock' => $currentStock,
            'available_stock' => $currentStock - $reserved,
        ]);
    }

    private function createMovement(InventoryStock $stock, InventoryItem $item, array $data): StockMovement
    {
        // Movement menjadi audit trail setiap perubahan stok.
        return StockMovement::create([
            'outlet_id' => $stock->outlet_id,
            'division_id' => $item->division_id,
            'inventory_item_id' => $item->id,
            'movement_type' => $data['movement_type'],
            'reference_type' => $data['reference_type'] ?? null,
            'reference_id' => $data['reference_id'] ?? null,
            'qty' => $data['quantity'],
            'unit' => $stock->unit ?: $item->base_unit,
            'stock_before' => $data['stock_before'],
            'stock_after' => $data['stock_after'],
            'created_by' => $data['user_id'] ?? null,
            'notes' => $data['notes'] ?? null,
        ]);
    }

    private function requiredInt(array $data, string $key): int
    {
        if (! isset($data[$key]) || (int) $data[$key] <= 0) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException("{$key} is required.");
        }

        return (int) $data[$key];
    }

    private function decimal(mixed $value, string $key): float
    {
        if (! is_numeric($value)) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException("{$key} must be numeric.");
        }

        return (float) $value;
    }

    private function result(InventoryStock $stock, ?StockMovement $movement, bool $duplicate = false, bool $noop = false): array
    {
        return [
            'stock' => $stock,
            'movement' => $movement,
            'duplicate' => $duplicate,
            'noop' => $noop,
        ];
    }
}

=== FILE: routes/api.php ===

<?php

use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| API Routes
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Here is where you can register API routes for your application. These
| routes are loaded by the RouteServiceProvider and all of them will
| be assigned to the "api" middleware group. Make something great!
|
*/

Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->get('/user', function (Request $request) {
    return $request->user();
});


=== FILE: routes/channels.php ===

<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Broadcast;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Broadcast Channels
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Here you may register all of the event broadcasting channels that your
| application supports. The given channel authorization callbacks are
| used to check if an authenticated user can listen to the channel.
|
*/

Broadcast::channel('App.Models.User.{id}', function ($user, $id) {
    return (int) $user->id === (int) $id;
});


=== FILE: routes/console.php ===

<?php

use Illuminate\Foundation\Inspiring;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Console Routes
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| This file is where you may define all of your Closure based console
| commands. Each Closure is bound to a command instance allowing a
| simple approach to interacting with each command's IO methods.
|
*/

Artisan::command('inspire', function () {
    $this->comment(Inspiring::quote());
})->purpose('Display an inspiring quote');

    Route::post('backend/recipes', [BackendRecipeController::class, 'store'])->name('backend.recipes.store');
    Route::get('backend/recipes/{recipe}', [BackendRecipeController::class, 'show'])->name('backend.recipes.show');
    Route::get('backend/recipes/{recipe}/edit', [BackendRecipeController::class, 'edit'])->name('backend.recipes.edit');
    Route::put('backend/recipes/{recipe}', [BackendRecipeController::class, 'update'])->name('backend.recipes.update');

    Route::get('backend/chats', [ConversationController::class, 'index'])->name('backend.chat.index');
    Route::get('backend/chats/{conversation}', [ConversationController::class, 'adminShow'])->name('backend.chat.show');
    Route::post('backend/chats/{conversation}/messages', [MessageController::class, 'adminSend'])->name('backend.chat.send');
    Route::get('backend/chats/{conversation}/messages', [MessageController::class, 'adminFetch'])->name('backend.chat.fetch');
});

// Halaman katalog publik.
Route::get('/beranda', [BerandaController::class, 'index'])->name('beranda');
Route::get('/products', [ProductController::class, 'catalog'])->name('product.index');
Route::get('/products/category/{category:slug}', [ProductController::class, 'category'])->name('product.category');
Route::get('/products/{product}', [ProductController::class, 'detail'])->name('product.detail');

// Area customer setelah login: akun, alamat, cart, checkout, order, payment, dan chat.
Route::middleware(['auth', 'is.customer'])->group(function () {
    // Route untuk menampilkan halaman akun customer
    Route::get('/customer/account', [CustomerController::class, 'account'])
        ->name('customer.account');

    // Route untuk mengupdate data akun customer
    Route::put('/customer/account', [CustomerController::class, 'updateAccount'])
        ->name('customer.account.update');

    // Address book menjadi sumber alamat pengiriman untuk checkout shipping.
    Route::get('/customer/addresses', [CustomerAddressController::class, 'index'])->name('customer.address.index');
    Route::get('/customer/addresses/create', [CustomerAddressController::class, 'create'])->name('customer.address.create');
    Route::post('/customer/addresses', [CustomerAddressController::class, 'store'])->name('customer.address.store');
    Route::get('/customer/addresses/{address}/edit', [CustomerAddressController::class, 'edit'])->name('customer.address.edit');
    Route::put('/customer/addresses/{address}', [CustomerAddressController::class, 'update'])->name('customer.address.update');
    Route::delete('/customer/addresses/{address}', [CustomerAddressController::class, 'destroy'])->name('customer.address.destroy');
    Route::patch('/customer/addresses/{address}/default', [CustomerAddressController::class, 'setDefault'])->name('customer.address.setDefault');

    // Cart masih berupa keranjang aktif customer, bukan order.
    Route::get('cart', [CartController::class, 'index'])->name('cart.index');
    Route::post('cart/add/{product}', [CartController::class, 'add'])->name('cart.add');
    Route::patch('cart/items/{cartItem}', [CartController::class, 'update'])->name('cart.update');
    Route::delete('cart/items/{cartItem}', [CartController::class, 'remove'])->name('cart.remove');
    Route::delete('cart/clear', [CartController::class, 'clear'])->name('cart.clear');
    // Checkout mengubah cart menjadi order, payment, dan shipment.
    Route::get('checkout', [CheckoutController::class, 'index'])->name('checkout.index');
    Route::post('checkout', [CheckoutController::class, 'process'])->name('checkout.process');
    // Endpoint AJAX RajaOngkir/Komerce untuk pilihan shipping di checkout dan address book.
    Route::get('rajaongkir/provinces', [RajaOngkirController::class, 'provinces'])->name('rajaongkir.provinces');
    Route::get('rajaongkir/cities', [RajaOngkirController::class, 'cities'])->name('rajaongkir.cities');
    Route::get('rajaongkir/districts', [RajaOngkirController::class, 'districts'])->name('rajaongkir.districts');
    Route::post('rajaongkir/cost', [RajaOngkirController::class, 'cost'])->name('rajaongkir.cost');
    // Riwayat order customer dan chat per-order.
    Route::get('orders', [OrderController::class, 'history'])->name('order.history');
    Route::get('orders/{order}/chat', [ConversationController::class, 'show'])->name('chat.show');
    Route::post('orders/{order}/chat/messages', [MessageController::class, 'send'])->name('chat.send');
    Route::get('orders/{order}/chat/messages', [MessageController::class, 'fetch'])->name('chat.fetch');
    Route::get('orders/{order}', [OrderController::class, 'detail'])->name('order.detail');
    // Payment manual customer: lihat status dan upload bukti transfer.
    Route::get('payments/{order}', [PaymentController::class, 'show'])->name('payment.show');
    Route::get('payments/{order}/upload-proof', [PaymentController::class, 'uploadProof'])->name('payment.uploadProof');
    Route::post('payments/{order}/upload-proof', [PaymentController::class, 'storeProof'])->name('payment.storeProof');
});
